# Change log

## Release v0.7.11 (2026-10-28)

- Publishes the verified local 0.7.11 output, combining the changes in local 0.7.3-0.7.11 below: Native/HD font selection and 640x480 output, movie drawing, story choices/map labels, compact names, marker alignment, complete Trust help, signed SP, room tags and fitted location banners. Original movie bytes and VGA support are retained.
- Full original-input xdelta patches, schema-2 manifest, source/target hashes and exact Retro Trans Automatic output verification are included. Stage 6 issue #1 remains unresolved; latest GPU/hardware appearance, one unidentified room tag and full-game review are still pending. See docs/releases/v0.7.11.md.

## 0.7.11 — measured location headers (2026-10-28)

- Corrected location-banner measurement: English labels now use the current Skies font advances instead of reserving eight logical pixels for every Latin byte. The right text anchor at x313 and text y22 position, original border pieces, repeated-body sizing and animation code are retained. The reported Summoning Facility label shrinks from 232 to 138 logical pixels; lettering and wording are unchanged.
- Verified all 256 location rows in both font banks (512 native measurements and 512 actual location geometry phases), including independent glyph/shadow bounds. All 2,984 other valid name-row/font cases exactly match the previous native measurement. Callee-saved registers and stack pass; only the original 278-byte measurement function changes. Every asset, including both font banks, is byte-exact 0.7.10.
- Local test disc: fifteen exact payload re-extractions, changed-sector EDC/ECC and full track preservation pass. No GitHub release. Final live GPU/hardware appearance, unidentified room tag 66, full-game review and the separate Stage 6 issue #1 remain pending.

## 0.7.10 — signed SP, compact notices and room-map names (2026-10-28)

- Fixed the original signed-number generator emitting unavailable Japanese-font +/minus/plus-minus glyphs in SP and other signed fields. It now emits Latin + and -; neutral displays +0. Magnitudes, caller field padding, shared queue/ring behavior and gameplay values are retained. Tested every signed byte -127..127 at field widths 0/2/3/4 in both font modes (2,040 native generation/parser cases); the legacy -128 magnitude edge is outside this gameplay proof.
- Replaced the overflowing Register Topic heading with Topic Log (within a 48-logical-pixel text field). Reworded the full-VMU notice into three short lines, preserving the requirement of at least eight free blocks. Both fonts pass native parser and independent glyph/shadow bounds. All 1,746 other name rows retain 0.7.9 bytes.
- Translated 33 room-selection name tags in ITM00 member 23, including Clovis and Arthur. Used original Skies 12x24 VQ pixels at a uniform one-third scale with a shared baseline and variable advances. Retained all portraits, room art, sprite geometry/anchors, texture dimensions and headers; all 25 other member payloads remain exact. Unidentified tag sprite 66 is preserved rather than guessed. Native texture PRS decoding and SDK-compatible archive offsets pass.
- Local 0.7.10 disc contains fifteen exact re-extracted payloads; changed-sector EDC/ECC and all 1,188,856,032 track bytes pass preservation checks. Main font banks and kernel remain unchanged. No GitHub release. Latest live UI/hardware testing, the unidentified tag and the separate Stage 6 issue #1 remain pending.

## 0.7.9 — complete Trust help and compact popup labels (2026-10-28)

- Source-reviewed all eleven Trust-command effects. Accelerate now shows movement +3 for one move; Awareness clearly negates the hit-rate and damage modifiers of the next side/rear attack on the selected unit. All descriptions preserve amounts, targets and durations and fit at most two lines, wrapped at whole words using the current font widths.
- Added compact aliases only in the narrow tactical popup (including Accel. and Aware), with padding that leaves room for three-digit dynamic values. Full command names in status screens and all parameter controls remain intact. All 220 Native/HD label/value cases and 22 complete-help parser cases pass actual SH-4 parsing and independent original font-mask bounds; native PRS decoding passes. Other name rows are byte-exact.
- Only D_NAMES.BIN changes from 0.7.8. Both font banks, executable, gameplay tables, all dialogue, movies and the other thirteen payloads retain their prior bytes. All fourteen payloads re-extract exactly, changed-sector EDC/ECC pass and all 1,188,856,032 track bytes pass preservation checks. Local test build/package only; no GitHub release. Live popup appearance remains pending. The separate Stage 6 corruption report in GitHub issue #1 remains unresolved and is not claimed fixed here.

## 0.7.8 — Trust/Skills square-marker alignment (2026-10-27)

- Aligned the two lower status text panels with their original square markers by moving text down two logical pixels (four pixels at native 640×480). Moves both draw origins and the original linked tile origins, so absolute-coordinate controls remain valid when the text is cleared and redrawn. Only the two exact embedded status constructors receive this adjustment; adjacent panels and other contexts retain their original geometry.
- Replaced the variable-width leading space before Skills with an explicit eight-logical-pixel heading indent, placing it five logical pixels farther right. Restored the original coordinate origin before its ability entries, preserving their relative positions and all wording. Trust's horizontal position, marker artwork, frames and font pixels remain unchanged.
- Passed twelve actual Native/HD constructor cases, all four linked-tile origins, four real clear/parser/object-position paths with repeated redraws, screenshot-derived marker/glyph bounds, unchanged ability rows and native PRS decoding. All 1,747 other non-sentinel name entries remain byte-exact 0.7.7. All fourteen payloads re-extract exactly; changed-sector EDC/ECC and all 1,188,856,032 track bytes pass preservation checks. Actual GPU/hardware appearance and full-game review remain pending. Local build/package only; no GitHub release.

## 0.7.7 — compact weapon names and event-movie titles (2026-10-27)

- Shortened thirteen weapon display entries and seven matching event-movie titles. The reported attacks now display as “Vanishing Shadow” and “Seven Flashes: Dragon Rift”; matching Rayblade/Zeifon/Hongshenguang movie entries use the same short forms. Full authored terms remain in the working translation and English name export. The English-only compact-name recipe records all twenty aliases and measured widths.
- Audited all 256 weapon entries and 80 movie titles against the unchanged Skies font. All fit conservative pen limits of 136 and 240 logical pixels respectively, with ink/shadow bounds checked separately. Passed native parsing for all 336 entries, five nested weapon/stat row fixtures and the original PRS decoder. All 1,728 other non-sentinel name rows remain byte-exact; the names builder preserves non-text tables and unused sentinels.
- Only D_NAMES.BIN changes. Executable, both font banks, stats/branching, story and battle dialogue, 0.7.6 choice/map fixes, VGA boot and original movie/audio bytes remain exact. All fourteen payloads re-extract exactly; changed-sector EDC/ECC and all 1,188,856,032 track bytes pass preservation checks. Native row tests use bounded caller/context fixtures; actual GPU appearance and navigation remain pending. Local test/package only; no GitHub release.

## 0.7.6 — separate story choices and native map lettering (2026-10-27)

- Fixed the reported “Let them pass Attack” list and displaced selection highlight. Dialogue reflow had treated selectable-row separators as prose line breaks. Restored the original option boundaries in all 961 translated choice commands (952 compiled streams changed), preserving wording, order, original VM prefixes, branch operands and source consumption. The thirteen overlong lists use compact Skies text, closing compact mode before each newline so the original sixteen-logical-pixel selection pitch remains intact. All 49,312 normal story streams remain byte-exact 0.7.5.
- Rebuilt all ten map names from the original 12×24 Skies pixels at one texture sample per native framebuffer pixel. Their separate atlas is now 512×128 ARGB4444, with a one-pixel dark edge and the original four alpha levels; it uses 128 KiB instead of 512 KiB. Added physical-pixel position snapping restricted to the English map-label texture list and IDs 17–26. Eighteen marker sprites, terrain/artwork, label anchors/visible sizes and caller-owned sprite data remain unchanged. Map-motion appearance still requires live confirmation; this change does not claim animation was the sole cause.
- Passed native lookup/formatting/parser/VM checks for all 961 lists, including distinct “Let them pass” and “Attack” rows at y16/y32; three actual scenario loader/decode cases; map PRS, all ten independent source-glyph comparisons, 448 story plus 21 map SDK lookups, native map registration/28 geometries, and 126 restricted draw-hook/ABI cases. Re-extracted all fourteen files exactly, checked changed-sector EDC/ECC and compared all 1,188,856,032 track bytes. Only the executable, story bank and map archive changed; both font banks, VGA boot, opening-movie fit and original movie/audio bytes retain 0.7.5 behavior. Physical hardware/non-VGA/full-game testing remain pending. Local build/package only; no GitHub release.

## 0.7.5 — local movie fit for the 640×480 font build (2026-10-27)

- Added a local xdelta package against the original Japanese BIN/CUE set: Track 3, Track 5 and CUE-to-GDI patches, with unchanged Tracks 1/2/4. All three full decode round trips and the real Retro Trans Tools complete-disc application match the tested 0.7.5 output. Includes instructions, source/target checksums and validation records. No GitHub release was created.
- Fixed the quarter-size opening/movie image reported in the experimental 0.7.4 build. The game's separate Sofdec position and size setters now map their original 320×240 coordinates into the 640×480 framebuffer. The 320×192 movies display at 640×384 with their original letterboxing. Movie video, audio, timing, container bytes and the removal of English movie overlays are retained without re-encoding.
- Changed only 28 executable bytes from the user's 0.7.4 rendering candidate. The complete font member, WIN00 archive, wording, wrapping, title font selector and render adapters are byte-exact that candidate. Native main letters preserve the original Skies 12×24 pixels/four alpha levels; compact status uses 8×16. Flycast 1× is now a real 640×480 render area; no emulator replacement textures are used.
- Passed actual application initialization/original setter execution for all 129 movie entries, fourteen drawing-adapter cases, 402 font raster cases, fourteen allocator cases and the title-selector checks. Visually checked opening playback/fit, title return and a normal save load through intermission into Touya status in an isolated Flycast VGA/1× profile. Runtime code and the complete font member match the current build; Lv/Exp/HP remain legible. Packaged 69 sector substitutions from 0.7.3; all fourteen files, changed-sector EDC/ECC and all 1,188,856,032 track bytes pass preservation checks. Physical hardware, non-VGA and full-game testing remain pending. Local test only; public VERSION stays 0.7.2.

## 0.7.4 — experimental full-resolution Native font (2026-10-27)

- Replaced the reduced 0.7.3 Native font with the original Skies 12×24 main glyphs and uniformly reduced 8×16 compact glyphs. Preserved source coverage levels, shared variable advances, wording and wrapping. Added 640×480 video initialization and uniform adapters for sprites, polygons and clipping rectangles, retaining logical UI positions. This fixes the underlying 320×240 rendering step that discarded thin font strokes despite 640×480 display output.
- Kept X Native / Y HD title selection and the unchanged HD bank. The user confirmed the font's improved 1× appearance. The delivered candidate remained in `work/output/skies-render-test/`; it was not a public release or a finalized build. Its separate movie drawing path still used half-size coordinates; fixed in 0.7.5. Physical Dreamcast and non-VGA rendering remain unverified.

## 0.7.3 — local Native / HD font selector (2026-10-27)

- Added a title-screen font choice: X selects Native (1x), Y selects the existing HD mode. Native is the cold-boot default; the choice lasts for the current session and does not write to the VMU. The indicator sits below the original copyright line and is removed when leaving the title. Both banks keep the same variable advances and existing dialogue wrapping.
- Retained the original Skies HD font bank byte-for-byte. Added uniformly area-filtered native glyphs with the same four coverage levels: small letters use one-third of the original 12x24 source size, medium letters one-half. Native text uses logical-size surfaces and nearest filtering, avoiding the thin-stroke loss seen when reducing the HD bank at 1x output. Five protected symbols retain their existing IDs and use native-size derivatives. No texture replacement or texture dimension above 1024 is introduced.
- Passed 804 native raster cases and eight allocation cases, plus actual native selector construction/parsing, button debounce, unchanged VM pointer/register/stack checks and zero additional surface allocations on repeated switches. Flycast at 640x480 confirms both title choices, Native carried through a normal save load into status, readable Lv/Exp/HP, and HD carried through New Game into dialogue. Read-only runtime checks match the complete two-bank font and executable code. Physical Dreamcast appearance and full-game/long-session coverage remain pending.
- Repacked WIN00 in compact SDK sector order within its original ISO allocation; all 89 non-font members remain exact. Packaged 621 sector substitutions, checked all fourteen extracted payloads, EDC/ECC and all 1,188,856,032 track bytes. The other twelve payloads, VGA support, boot area and original opening/demo movies remain unchanged. Local test only; no GitHub release. Public VERSION stays 0.7.2.

- Publishes the integrated VGA code/boot flag from local 0.7.1, credited to Rolly (SEGA-SKY). Retains its non-conflicting English executable, native Skies font, whole-word wrapping, menu and high-detail map work. The 96 native video cases and isolated VGA/composite cold-boot telemetry retain their actual tested identities; physical Dreamcast NTSC/PAL and full gameplay testing remain pending.

Restores MOV055.SFD (opening) and MOV072.SFD (demo) byte-for-byte from the original Japanese retail disc, removing all added English subtitles, captions, title/UI and ending-title edits. Restores original audio/video/container/timing together, without re-encoding. Previous translated movie recipes and records remain historical.
- Reverts 25,099 movie sectors while retaining all twelve non-movie payloads and the VGA-enabled boot area. Packages a new complete-disc Retro Trans Tools solution with three verified original-input xdelta patches and three unchanged tracks. No complete game images or extracted Japanese scripts are distributed.
- Translation remains a work in progress with 503 held/untranslated story rows. This update adds no new dialogue translation and makes no claim of complete human proofreading or route coverage.

## 0.7.1 — local VGA compatibility test (2026-10-26)

- Merged all 497 changed executable bytes from Rolly (SEGA-SKY)'s retail VGA patch v1.0 into the English 0.7.0 / local 0.1.44 payloads. Original-byte checks find no overlap with translation/font/menu changes. Added the boot VGA compatibility flag; retained the Japanese region selection and existing English disc artwork. The separate trial-disc patch is not used.
- Added reproducible merge and disc packaging tools with pinned source hashes. Actual SH-4 checks pass for both video initialization and register commit across all four cable IDs and twelve flag combinations: 96 cases, 5,954 instructions. VGA paths set progressive sync/clock registers; non-VGA initialization reaches the unchanged original fallback. Hardware, BIOS and full-game behavior are outside this bounded fixture proof.
- Packaged five sector substitutions. All fourteen game files re-extract exactly; the thirteen non-executable payloads, English wording, font, map labels, movies and audio remain unchanged. Full 1,188,856,032-byte track comparison and changed-sector EDC/ECC pass. Thirty-four historical proof identities retain their actual tested executable hashes.
- Isolated cold boots in Flycast with VGA and composite selected both reach the patched executable. Read-only Lua telemetry spans 1,200 vblanks per case; VGA reports 640×480, progressive sync control 0x100, sync totals 0x020c0359 and the VGA pixel clock. Composite selects the game's TV timing. These are NTSC-configured telemetry checks, without visual inspection or gameplay progression.
- Local test only; no GitHub release. Cold-boot the new GDI with VGA selected; physical Dreamcast VGA/HDMI-adapter, NTSC/PAL and non-VGA regression testing remains pending. Public VERSION remains 0.7.0.

## 0.7.0 — first public release

- Publishes the verified cumulative local 0.1.44 payloads as v0.7.0. Game track bytes are unchanged from that local build.
- Adds a public README and release notes modeled on retro-trans/SRW-Z, English-only source exports, current font notices and a Retro Trans Tools schema-v2 complete-disc release. Tracks 3/5 are patched, Tracks 1/2/4 copied unchanged, and a descriptor delta converts the original CUE to the verified English GDI.
- Release remains a work in progress: 503 held/untranslated story rows, independent review, complete routes and newest live map/menu/VRAM checks remain unfinished. Native and preservation evidence is not described as full gameplay testing.
- No complete game tracks, extracted Japanese scripts, standalone game fonts or local captures are included in the public repository/release. Every public delta must pass full source-to-target SHA-256 round-trip verification before publication.

## 0.1.44 — sharper, higher-contrast world-map names

- Rebuilt all ten map place names with the original Skies Latin glyph pixels and variable advances. Replaced the historical downsampled supplied bitmap and dim blue-gray lettering with bright cream text, original four-level transparency and a thin dark edge. Glyph shapes and wording remain intact; no individual stretching, contour reconstruction or emulator replacement textures.
- Integrated a separate 1024×256 ARGB4444 rectangular twiddled font texture. Each label has four texture pixels per original display pixel on both axes, preserving the 16-pixel display height, vertical anchors and horizontal call-point centers. Wider English cells remain centered. Added texture storage is 524,288 bytes. Removed only the old text cells from the original 512×512 marker atlas; all eighteen marker sprites and all seventeen terrain/art member payloads remain exact.
- Expanded the map NJTL to two textures and its archive to twenty-one members. The new label PVR follows the marker PVR; the original authoring filename list is retained unchanged as the final member. Repacked in SDK compact-sector order within the existing ISO allocation, with 38,653 spare bytes. Executable code, normal dialogue font, every translation payload and the other thirteen build files are byte-exact 0.1.43.
- Passed independent original Skies VQ glyph/alpha comparisons for every label, protected-pixel/geometry preservation, all twenty-one native archive lookups, native two-texture NJTL relocation/registration and twenty-eight native sprite bounds/normalized-UV calculations. Both original-game PRS decodes and the native 1024×256 ARGB4444 texture-info case pass with guards/ABI checks. I/O/allocation fixtures are bounded; actual map routing, upload/VRAM, GPU blending/filtering, dialogue fade and screen overlap remain for live checking.
- Packaged 2306 sector substitutions; all fourteen files re-extract exactly. Changed-sector EDC/ECC and full 1,188,856,032-byte track comparison pass. Thirty-three prior proof identities retained. Fresh-boot 0.1.44, load a normal save and inspect map labels with and without dialogue. No public release or prior-build replacement.

The initial suspicion that these surname forms were mistakes was resolved by the existing translation conventions.

Fixtures and pre-GPU frame limits are documented; live cursor/highlight appearance, interaction and navigation remain pending.

Twelve other payloads remain exact, including the font, reflowed story/battle dialogue, library, album and movies. Thirty-one historical proofs retain their actual tested identities. ## Font research — compare the original Skies of Arcadia disc

- Read the USA Disc 1 CUE/BIN in E:\Games without modifying it. Located the executable's 96 Latin glyphs at file offset 0x360cd4 through the original ASCII loader at 0x8c02a1b8. Each 72-byte glyph is a 6x12 grid of VQ indices representing 12x24 pixels. The earlier exploratory four-bit interpretation was incorrect and its temporary previews were discarded.
- Executed the original VQ codebook generator at 0x8c02ad8e: all 256 entries/2,048 bytes match the independent block decoder, with guards, saved registers and stack preserved across 9,381 integer SH-4 instructions. All 96 glyphs reconstruct their source bytes exactly. The actual codebook has alpha levels 0/85/187/255, matching the supplied atlas; extra edge shades are not the explanation.
- Compared all 95 printable ASCII glyphs at matching cell coordinates: 18 are identical and 77 differ. The embedded Skies font has 18.205% more total alpha coverage than the supplied bitmap. Saved the original decoded font, an offline three-way specimen and per-glyph measurements under work/ui/skies-font-comparison, with a compact record in docs/skies-font-comparison.json.
- Rayblade additionally reconstructs contours, changes side bearings/advances and point-samples coverage when reducing dialogue surfaces. The specimen normalizes mask scale; it does not simulate either game's GPU. Skies' actual dialogue usage/filter/blend/scale still needs tracing, so the entire screenshot difference is not attributed to one cause. ## 0.1.38 — fix archive loading corruption after New Game

- Reproduced the reported 0.1.37 failure from read-only captured game RAM. The original SDK's compact directory uses a first sector followed by cumulative rounded member lengths, rather than every absolute AFS offset. Its entire 777-member ADV00 length list matches the captured native table. The English letter had been moved out of sequence and enlarged, so member 380 was fetched at byte 22,067,200 instead of 22,063,104. Reversing the one native pointer relocation makes all 80 captured bad bytes match that wrong archive location exactly. Pixel data was interpreted as NJTL metadata and the resulting relocation walk added 0x8c82f2e8 at twelve-byte intervals through game memory. Reversing that pattern in an analysis copy restores both scenario hooks, the valid 0x8cd55700 English heap pointer and the 128 KiB allocation literal. No running process memory was changed.
- Audited all eight modified AFS archives and repaired six incompatible layouts: ADV00, ALBUM, EFFECT, WMAP, STAFF and D_SYSTEM. All 1,525 member payloads, member IDs, translations, pictures and compressed streams remain exact 0.1.37. WIN00 and D_SCE already satisfy the native cumulative-sector layout and remain byte-exact, as do the executable, normal font and other eight unaffected game payloads. WMAP/ALBUM use two additional sectors of zero prefix to retain their original file extents; no content was shortened or recompressed.
- Executed actual SDK validation, directory traversal and offset/length calculation for all 2,063 members in eight archives: 2,869,127 SH-4 instructions including the captured failing lookup. Corrected physical offsets, padded read bounds, guards, registers and source buffers pass. Only the 40-byte file-info copy endpoint uses a bounded fixture. D_SYSTEM's SDK slot is a generic fixture; its actual registration/use remains unproved. This is not a live fresh-game, graphics or full-playthrough result.
- Packaged local build 0.1.38 with 28,736 sector substitutions over 0.1.37 and 56,409 locations changed from original. All fourteen payloads re-extract exactly, changed-sector EDC/ECC and the complete 1,188,856,032-byte track comparison pass, ISO allocations remain unchanged, and twenty-six historical native proof identities are retained under their actual tested identities. Added a cumulative-sector compatibility gate for every archive in builds starting with 0.1.38, preventing this packaging error from recurring.
- Test the new GDI from a fresh boot, choose New Game and continue past the memory screen to first dialogue. A save state from the corrupted run contains overwritten code and must not be resumed for this check; VMU save files are unchanged. Computer control was not resumed. Independent meaning, later asset loading/navigation, save/load, font appearance and full-game translation review remain unfinished. No new translation credit, original-dump/prior-build changes or release.

## Working translation — native ANIME resource boundaries

The schema follows the original reader at 8c0241e0: a count of six-byte vector entries, then sixteen-byte record headers and two counted word streams, ending with two zero counts. Opcode 74/subcommand 2 registers this data; its positive displacement is relative to command+2 and rounded up to an even address. It is not a callback registration.

## 0.1.3 — in development

- Started glossary-first text extraction and library discovery from the original Dreamcast data.
- Built a source-linked working glossary of 501 entries: 138 character labels, 114 mech labels, 140 distinct weapon labels, and 109 terms. Added profile-based role/personality metadata and explicit uncertainty notes; English spellings remain editorial drafts.
- Completed the first English pass of all 13 D_NAMES text banks: 1,489 nonempty source records prepared in English (971 explicitly authored records, 387 glossary-derived labels, and 131 existing ASCII/control/placeholder records). Preserved 92 unused binary-table sentinels. Includes menus, Trust Commands and tooltips, mission conditions, locations, event topics, ship rooms, chapter titles, weapon names, and gallery captions. Development placeholders remain explicitly labeled rather than replaced with invented content.
- Added a Latin text encoder and an expanded D_NAMES component builder. Relocated 1,748 text pointers into an appended English pool while preserving every other original byte and all unused sentinels. The component expands from 40,685 to 63,662 decoded bytes and recompresses to 33,930 bytes. Loader capacity and disc insertion checks are recorded below; game layout remains pending.
- Added a compatible PRS encoder and checked exact control-bit/endianness golden streams, overlapping copies, 8 KiB history boundaries, full printable ASCII, mode switches, formatting controls and unsupported-character rejection.
- Built a relocatable English library component: all 416 root pointers, 257 physical records including empty records, and 67 conditional unlock nodes rebuilt and reparsed. Preserved profile metadata and every original profile byte. The component is 121,903 decoded / 65,288 compressed bytes; native library loader capacity and screen layout remain pending.
- Executed the original SH-4 PRS decoder against both English components. Exact returned sizes and output, preserved registers/stack, and unchanged destination guards passed (1,866,424 instructions for names; 3,544,746 for library). Confirmed the native name-table loader allocates 65,536 bytes, leaving 1,874 bytes spare.
- Built a local English menu/name disc with the integrated HD font. Relocated D_NAMES to verified zero padding at LBA 549000, updated both byte orders of ISO extent/size fields, and regenerated 62 sectors across Tracks 3 and 5. All three patched files re-extract exactly, all changed sectors pass EDC/ECC, and unrelated ISO directory entries are unchanged.
- Booted the test disc in the existing Flycast installation to the opening movie. Read-only inspection confirms the entire 63,662-byte English name component, HD font and patched renderer match the build in emulated RAM. English menu readability/layout remains unverified; library and story are not inserted in this disc.
- Corrected 16 manually identified kanji after comparing enlarged bitmaps with repeated script phrases. Regenerated the local source corpus; confirmed no decoded lines changed in the already drafted library or s_001. Kept the two anomalous embedded-control blocks unresolved rather than treating their bytes as prose.
- Identified and extracted the reference library from WIN00.AFS member 52, D_PAPER.BIN. Its 416 pointers resolve to 181 nonempty physical profiles (92 character and 89 mech profiles), with 180 distinct payloads because one Garna profile is duplicated at a separate address.
- Completed the first English library draft: 1,549 nonempty rows and 67 preserved structural blank rows. Preserved hard line boundaries and story-unlock condition stacks. Translation meaning review, layout fitting, reinsertion, and gameplay checks remain pending.
- Corrected misleading library-name associations: profile 94 is the deceased queen Ipha; the record shared by pointers 101/102 describes Lenis, not Grauzer. Kept physical profile IDs stable and recorded identity overrides.
- Added English-only exports and structural checks for source fingerprints, control tokens, glyph coverage, conditional unlocks, line boundaries, and the implemented ASCII font repertoire. Name/term consistency checks currently flag no unmatched entries; this is not a substitute for meaning review.
- Excluded 92 unused name-table pointers that target the next binary table from prose decoding.
- Indexed and hash-verified all 447 original story-script members. Added candidate extraction for the observed inline text command and five style values: 50,626 text blocks / 80,755 physical rows, with 26,550 distinct encoded payloads. These counts do not prove complete VM text coverage. Kept four review items explicit: two unrecognized signatures in binary commands and two copies of an anomalous inline control sequence.

Preserved concealed speaker labels, formatting controls, source interruptions and row boundaries. Meaning review, complete script relocation and game insertion remain pending. The retained heart symbol requires a font slot before a playable build.

The original 50,626 first-page blocks / 80,755 rows were unchanged during page-coverage migration; the prepared s_001 source remains unchanged. Full VM/opcode coverage is still unproven.

Resolved the two previously garbled inline-control cases without assigning a false glyph; two binary-looking signature matches remain explicit review items.

## 0.1.2 — experimental local build

- Integrated a Latin-only font into the native renderer, using 4x rerasterized glyph contours in the existing MOJI archive member.
- Kept logical text sizes, line spacing, and variable-width advances unchanged. Physical text surfaces use up to 4x detail per axis, capped at 1024 pixels per edge. A 512-pixel-wide logical surface uses 2x horizontal detail.
- Added a 2 MiB text-surface budget target and allocation fallback to lower detail when memory is tight; native-size allocation remains the minimum.
- Patched surface allocation, clearing, PVR headers, cache flush length, and queued upload size. Preserved the script's append-text entry point.
- Passed 220 offline HD glyph cases and seven allocation/header/append/flush/upload cases, executing 3,448,986 SH-4 instructions.
- Rebuilt the local disc with 44 changed sectors and validated MODE1 EDC/ECC. Re-extracted both patched files and verified exact equality.
- Confirmed the loaded renderer and font payload match the local build in Flycast. Passed an initial live dialogue check: the used 512x64 logical surface renders into 1024x256 pixels while its display sprite remains 512x64. Captured 944 logical pixel cells containing finer subpixel detail. No visible clipping or oversized text was observed on the tested page.
- Archived the dialogue screenshot, CPU text surfaces, and validation notes under `work/ui/`. Full English pages, other UI layouts, long sessions, and hardware remain unverified.
- No emulator texture replacement is used. This is an experimental font build, not a translated game: original Japanese text is incompatible with the Latin-only font.

## 0.1.1 — experimental local build

- Implemented variable-width Latin advances and removed empty left bearings in the native glyph renderer.
- Passed 76 offline SH-4 rendering/ABI checks. Rebuilt and re-extracted the local disc to verify the patched executable.
- Generated 4x Latin font source atlases. These were source assets only, not integrated into this build.
- Gameplay validation remains pending.

## 0.1.0 — initial investigation

- Read the project rules and identified the supplied five-track Dreamcast dump.
- Prioritized a variable-width font and, if supported by the renderer, font textures with four times the width and height at the same on-screen size.
- Added a read-only CUE/ISO inventory tool supporting the split high-density data tracks. It defaults to a dry run.
- Added exclusions for original disc data, local Japanese source, translation working files, and generated output.
